# Skills · L · part 63

1,050 entries, lint to linting-and-formatting. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **lint**: Health-check the vault's LLM wiki — a mechanical script pass (schema, broken links, duplicate basenames, orphans, index coverage, hot/index size, stale pages, layout paths) plus judgment checks within a read budget (contradictions, freshness violations, important concepts without pages). Reports first; fixes only what the user approves. Use when the user asks to lint, check, audit or clean up the vault/wiki, or asks what is broken or stale. ([erayozenn/BigHead-2ndBrain](https://github.com/erayozenn/BigHead-2ndBrain/tree/HEAD/skills/lint) · ★1)
- **lint**: Run the FACET lint suite and fix what it reports — schema violations, @facet: tags with no record, and active records with no source anchor. Use when the user asks to check, verify, validate, or lint FACET records, when FACET CI fails, or before committing changes that touch records or anchored source. ([facet-framework/core](https://github.com/facet-framework/core/tree/HEAD/skills/lint) · ★1)

- **lint**: Run a health check on all wiki pages. - **lint**: Health check for the wiki knowledge base. Scans wiki/ for dead links (wikilinks pointing to non-existent pages), orphan pages (pages no other page links to), unsynced index (pages that exist on disk but aren't registered in wiki/index.md), and unresolved knowledge conflicts. Triggered by /lint, /scan, /health, or natural-language requests like "check my wiki health", "audit the knowledge base", "find orphans". Read-only by default — produces a structured report and asks the user before applying any fixes. ([fenghaitao/ObsidianVault](https://github.com/fenghaitao/ObsidianVault/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/lint) · ★1)

- **lint**: Runs three ordered quality-gate buckets — format (fix + re-stage), lint (check-only → auto-fix → hard-stop), typecheck (project-wide → hard-stop) — each discovered independently from declared project scripts; notifies and skips missing buckets; fail-fast on hard-stop. TRIGGER when: user says 'run lint', 'lint the code', 'check lint', 'run format', 'typecheck'. ([fslzrr/skills](https://github.com/fslzrr/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lint) · ★1)
- **lint**: Lints a bounded vault scope for mechanical health: broken or orphan links, placement drift, missing frontmatter, wiki index and log sync, MOC coverage, stale pages and link form (`[label](URL)` for external, wikilinks for internal, no `obsidian://` note links). Use for vault lint, note health or link checks. Quality review belongs to audit, per-claim checks to verify. ([GoBeromsu/secondbrain-skills](https://github.com/GoBeromsu/secondbrain-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lint) · ★1)
